# Sub-Head: 13_Finishing_Works
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[13.1.1] CPWD DAR 2019 Vol 2 13_Finishing_Works — Item 13.1.1
  Resource: [0101] Bhisti (Labour)
  Published Coefficient: 0.9200 day per 10.0 sqm
  Derived Productivity: 10.87 sqm/worker-day | 1.36 sqm/hr
  Derivation: 10.0 sqm / 0.9200 worker-days = 10.87 sqm/worker-day (1.36 sqm/hr across 8h shift)
  Evidence: CPWD DAR 2019 Vol 2 Item 13.1.1
